## Artifacts and Notes

### M104-MANUAL-ATTEMPT-6-7 — working audio and confirmed focus/announcement defects

The owner corrected the audio route and confirmed that Narrator was speaking through the connected headphones. In the exact headed manual harness, the observer heard target-URL guidance, the Target and generation mode heading, Analyze, the URL validation message, approximate Analyze-start content, Finding `finding-0`, its level-three detail heading and State content. The observer initially described two narrators and difficulty distinguishing later phrases. Read-only process inspection found one Windows `Narrator` process, not two. The observed Spanish control-role phrases and English page content are consistent with one Narrator switching language presentation; the observer later attributed the uncertainty to those two voices and considered the interaction fine. Do not treat the earlier non-confirmations as application failures.

The exact first session did not document completion, provider/model/no-call selection context or Reopen before its 15-minute ceiling. The harness emitted settled teardown after twelve owned HTTP 200 responses, zero external requests and zero page errors; its nonzero exit records only the deliberate ceiling. Fresh base verification found no owned process/listener or scratch residue. App SHA-256 remained `7673E4801642C70B9A9B478B5F796B9FE458472A81AD305341C0D01570E8A24D`; UI-test SHA-256 remained `F603C509E79F3356BD6EBA6BF645D67721892BAADE66CC5BD122BFA98560C6CB`.

A fresh continuation first produced an invalid completion observation because Narrator was off; discard it. After the owner explicitly restarted Narrator and confirmed speech, selecting `finding-0` automatically spoke only `Finding finding-0`. Down-Arrow reading could reach the visibly present run/provider text, but the automatic selection did not include it. Reopen of `m104-manual-01`, with no navigation keys pressed afterward, spoke `M1-04 UI contract` rather than the operation result. These are actual heard failures against REQ-A11Y-004/010 and L4.4. The continuation was interrupted after the decisive observations; zero owned runtime processes and zero port-5173 listeners remained. The primary's first ad hoc scratch digest used a noncanonical serializer and was rejected safely. The authoritative absolute-Path/Kind/Length/Hash inventory was 15 entries, 13 files, 2,527,253 bytes and digest `473BBD8A42594D43FAFD53DFDD1828BB02C03DFF8EA576F7C01E255546E3AFCC`.

The first recovery worker stopped twice without mutation: attempt 1 carried historical pre-remediation App/test hashes, and corrected attempt 2 received the noncanonical digest. Both leases closed compliantly with no changes. After primary independently reproduced the canonical digest, the workflow's fresh-instance route assigned `M104-NARRATOR-RECOVERY-02`. That worker matched the exact inventory and removed/recreated only `temp/m104-ui`; all three scratch roots are empty, the 332-entry runtime digest remains `E3FCE03453B560FCB9F45FC9325DC086F71579DFF21FA906142D2EFE4F1D2D28`, all seven protected hashes are unchanged, and zero owned process/listener remains. Lease `M1-04-20260831-01-narrator-recovery-fresh-01`, digest `658bcf2eebf7dbbfc83810c68126cd1fb905dc546900461659ce3f77a639f5ef`, closed compliantly with receipt `bded4f5bb248d93a984e1d05d852ea736830988c6c74c05d43352873bf1dbde4`.

### M104-NARRATOR-FIX-PREFLIGHT-01 — focus and description regression

The persistent test worker inspected App, RunResults, styles and the 38-test boundary read-only and returned `REGRESSION`. App already blocks overlap synchronously through `reservation.current`, so native busy disabling is not the operation owner; both capable buttons can preserve focus while exposing an ARIA-disabled busy state. RunResults already renders the selected State, run ID, exact provider triple and no-call statement, but the programmatically focused detail heading has no accessible description relationship to that text. Existing busy assertions require native disablement and therefore encode the focus defect; the keyboard selection case checks focus and shared status text but misses the heading description.

The accepted Red boundary is one coherent test-file-only change: preserve native disabled behavior for absent callbacks; while busy require capable Analyze/Reopen buttons to remain natively focusable, expose `aria-disabled="true"`, retain the busy description and issue no overlapping callback; prove the activated Analyze/Reopen button remains `document.activeElement` during and after representative settlements; extend the existing Finding keyboard case so the focused detail heading's accessible description contains State, run ID, mode/provider/model and no-call text while preserving the shared status and sibling evidence. The helper, dependencies, CSS, service/provider boundary and extra live regions remain forbidden. Actual Narrator revalidation remains mandatory after Green.

### M104-NARRATOR-RED-ACCEPTED — operation focus and detail description

The test worker changed only `tests/target-results-ui.test.ts`: two stale busy assertions now require capable buttons to remain natively enabled plus `aria-disabled="true"`; one case covers keyboard Analyze and mouse Reopen focus through held work, reentry and representative settlement; the existing keyboard Finding case now requires the focused detail heading's accessible description to contain State, run ID, local/Ollama/model and no-call text. All other tests and assertions remain. Test SHA-256 is `B16215E7D598B17EB64E75DA27B2523D3A0F313207D8322C608B4946B2B12E7F`; the helper remains `93BB2312EB0A62569075F7CCAF3A617EEF6C5B5DB7902EC8994DC76ACD160DB8`.

One exact focused run executed 39 tests: 36 passed and exactly three failed. The reservation case found capable Analyze still natively disabled; the new operation case found Analyze no longer active while held; the Finding case found no `State: unprocessed` accessible description on the focused detail heading. There were zero cancellations/skips, unrelated failures, external requests or page errors. Syntax and independent strict typechecking exited 0. Chromium `151.0.7922.34` emitted twelve owned HTTP 200 responses and settled teardown; all three scratch roots are empty, the runtime digest and three accepted PNGs are unchanged. Lease `M1-04-20260831-01-narrator-red-01`, digest `a38e732cf1542d4c1e6e8824e0f02f0297dddd8e072d095d05785752ad80c8fa`, closed compliantly with receipt `3dcbbeda3595ea2fa4458a291ffb4a07bf8ac2a58de04a4b495e6fc4f1654e6e`. This is accepted Red; Green must leave the test and helper unchanged.

### M104-NARRATOR-GREEN-CONFLICT-01 — Playwright enabled semantics

The first frontend Green attempt stopped with no diff because its packet omitted required Packet v2/frontend-visual fields; its lease closed compliantly. ### M104-REFACTOR-REVIEW-001 — synchronous reentry correction selected

Fresh `critical_reviewer` inspection returned `REVISE` with one Major implementation/test gap and one Minor evidence typo. The Minor is corrected above: RunResults is unchanged at the complete 64-character identity `BD76F098FA8172E2511FBF1FDEC531DB6B8DEE71EDAF18AFBA34A11E97C05AB7`.

The Major is a behavior-preservation defect introduced by the extraction. `TargetAnalysisForm` and `ReopenRunForm` use the render-lagging `busy` prop as their first submission guard. During a collaborator's same-turn reentry, App's reservation is already held but `busy` can still be false; an invalid second form therefore publishes its field error before its App callback reaches the authoritative reservation check. ## OD-026 and visible UI simplification amendment — M104-UI-SIMPLIFICATION-02

This current amendment supersedes only the earlier live literals that required a Reopen form, manual Run ID validation, reopen collaborator/admission, the two-heading setup introduction, the long target notice, or a pre-validation `Choose Local or Groq.` helper. Every earlier checkpoint remains historical evidence at its recorded source identity. The immutable run/scan contract, provider disclosures, Analyze intent, admission validation, busy ownership, complete/incomplete result projection, Finding selection, result limitations, untrusted-text rendering, service repository reads, and persisted Run ID evidence remain unchanged.

The accepted rendered contract is:

- one page-level `h1` named `Analyze a page`; no visible `A11y Evidence Lab` or `Target and generation mode` heading;
- exact target notice: `Use a public HTTPS page you are permitted to analyze. Private, authenticated, and hostile pages aren’t supported.` at entry and completed results;
- no `Choose Local or Groq.` text before validation; after an Analyze submission with no selected mode, the same text is a visible, associated and announced error;
- the selected Local or Groq disclosure remains visible and exact after selection, with Local still visibly recommended but not preselected;
- no manual Run ID field, Reopen action, reopen capability notice, reopen error, deep link, reload restoration, or recent-run UI; and
- Run ID remains visible in result provenance, while durable service/storage reads remain outside this client slice.

Production responsibility and dependency direction remain direct. `App.tsx` owns only Analyze async reservation/settlement, result publication, selection, focus and announcements. `RunControls.tsx` owns the one controlled target/mode form and its validation/disclosure. `run-admission.ts` owns only the React-free Analyze response envelope and identity validation. `styles.css` removes only the now-unused Reopen-form rule and keeps failed/interrupted result styling. `RunResults.tsx`, client entry, server/service/repository/domain source, package/configuration, fixtures, and scanner source are forbidden. Cohesion disposition is planned `REFACTORED` because removing the deferred branch makes each existing module smaller without adding an abstraction.

Use one coherent frontend-visual TDD slice. A `test_worker` first owns only `tests/target-results-ui.test.ts` and `tests/helpers/m104-ui-harness.ts`: remove obsolete reopen transport/assertions, preserve every applicable Analyze/result/focus/untrusted-content assertion, and add behavior-level assertions for the exact heading, notice, validation-only mode error, and absence of Run ID/Reopen controls. The accepted Red must execute the actual current components and fail only because the old visible/source contract remains. A separate `frontend_code_worker` then owns only `src/client/App.tsx`, `src/client/RunControls.tsx`, `src/client/run-admission.ts`, and `src/client/styles.css`, runs the accepted tests unchanged, reaches minimum Green, and may perform only the direct removal/refactor described above. ## Results-presentation amendment — M104-RESULTS-PRESENTATION-03

The owner reviewed the complete synthetic result on 2026-09-01 and selected a bounded presentation correction: the result must lead with the page, outcome, provider configuration, exact supported-rule coverage, complete grouped Findings and distinct scanner observations rather than the aggregate's implementation metadata. This is an M1-04 presentation change, not a domain-contract, persistence or M1-05 integration change.

The controlling authorities are M1-04; `REQ-SCAN-005`–`REQ-SCAN-007`; `REQ-EVID-004`, `REQ-EVID-007`–`REQ-EVID-011`; `REQ-UX-002`–`REQ-UX-005`, `REQ-UX-010`–`REQ-UX-014`; `REQ-A11Y-001`–`REQ-A11Y-004`, `REQ-A11Y-009` and `REQ-A11Y-010`; ADR-0012; SPEC-001 and SPEC-008. The canonical parent states remain `running`, `completed` and `failed`; the Finding state remains `unprocessed`; ScannerReviewObservations retain native `incomplete`; valid-zero, coverage failure, unavailable facts, immutable provider context and no-provider-call behavior remain unchanged. No friendly status alias may create another lifecycle model.

The visible contract is:

- At the point of interpretation, keep one concise limitation that names the current top-level-page boundary, exact three supported concerns, unsupported target classes and non-certification boundary. Do not repeat two long notices.
- Lead the completed result with the normalized analyzed page, `completed` state, total Findings, scanner-review observation count, Local/Groq provider and exact model, and the statement that no provider call was attempted.
- Keep exact per-rule coverage programmatically associated and understandable. The concise default summary may omit null buckets from its prose only when the full four-bucket values, including `Not reported`, remain available in an accessible technical disclosure.
- Keep every Finding visible in one rule-grouped keyboard-operable list. Each entry retains its exact stable ID, rule through its group and accessible name, concise target summary, canonical state and selection/detail action. Complete raw locators remain available in technical detail and are never used as identity.
- Lead selected Finding detail with its stable ID, canonical state, concise target summary, run provider/model context and actual no-call statement. Show a small rule-specific evidence summary for interpretation. Preserve every scanner-emitted check, locator, unavailability reason and exact native measurement inside a collapsed native `details` element labelled `Technical scanner evidence`.
- Keep ScannerReviewObservations visibly and semantically separate. Each stable observation summary exposes its global index, rule, native `incomplete` state, target summary and concise incomplete reason. Expanding an observation shows this concise interpretation first; its complete scanner evidence remains in a nested `Technical scanner evidence` disclosure.
- Put the complete run and scan provenance record—run ID, versions, times, revision, browser profile, viewport, locale, readiness, timeout, booleans, cleanup and contrast profile—inside one native `Technical run details` disclosure. Nothing is deleted, rounded, converted into a link or rendered as markup.

Reuse audit `M104-RESULTS-PRESENTATION-03-REUSE-001`: `RunResults` and its local evidence helpers are `EXTEND`; `App` is `EXTEND` only for the result-limitation copy; `styles.css` is `EXTEND` for the new hierarchy and disclosures. `RunControls`, admission, domain validation, service, persistence, fixtures, dependencies and build configuration are `REUSE_AS_IS`. No new component module, design system, state store, route, dependency or abstraction is justified. `RunResults` remains responsible for projecting one admitted run and local selection semantics; `App` remains responsible for orchestration, announcements and the outer results region. Dependency and runtime-call edges do not change. A bounded local refactor inside `RunResults.tsx` may add pure presentation helpers and focused local components when they make concise versus technical evidence responsibilities explicit.

This is one `frontend-visual` application-source work slice at S3 risk because it combines untrusted external strings, evidence completeness, assistive-technology relationships and focus behavior. TDD preflight and Red belong to a fresh `test_worker`, with write scope limited to `tests/target-results-ui.test.ts`; the helper remains protected unless preflight proves one exact transport gap and primary updates this plan before a lease. Separate Green/Refactor belongs to `frontend_code_worker`, with write scope limited to `src/client/App.tsx`, `src/client/RunResults.tsx` and `src/client/styles.css`; the accepted test is protected. Every write turn requires a fresh primary-opened and terminally closed lease. The implementation worker must report cohesion as `RETAINED`, `REFACTORED` or `RECONCILE`.

The Red contract must prove that the prior full-field default projection fails the new hierarchy while preserving complete accessible data. It covers completed nonzero, valid zero, failed, unavailable facts and observation cases; complete item/group presence; exact coverage; default-collapsed technical disclosures; full content after expansion; stable names and selection; focus return and announcement context; inert adversarial text; unrestricted axe; and 320 CSS-pixel reflow. It must not assert one decorative layout, exact whitespace, raw DOM structure or a second state vocabulary. The focused command remains the existing M1-04 browser suite and task-owned transport. Green must pass the accepted test unchanged, independent strict typecheck and production build. Final verification runs the complete existing product suite sequentially before the UI suite, then typecheck and build, followed by a test-boundary audit, fresh S3 review and a different integrated review.

Real-browser evidence samples the rich completed nonzero state at 1280×720, 320×800 and actual Chrome-menu 200% zoom. It checks default information hierarchy, disclosure operation, focus visibility, focus return and horizontal overflow. The screen-reader smoke is limited to the changed result path: Results heading and summary, one grouped Finding, selection/provider/no-call announcement, one technical disclosure, one distinct observation and predictable return. It does not repeat the complete MVP matrix or make a support claim.

### M104-ANALYZE-RESULTS-PRESENTATION-REPLACEMENT-08 — OD-027 owner-selected replacement

**Workflow:** `M1-04-20260901-09`. **TDD:** Applicable. **Risk:** S3 because the slice changes untrusted retained-data presentation, canonical-to-visible projection, provider-boundary copy, async lifecycle announcements, focus movement, and the core keyboard/screen-reader path. The implementation changes no canonical record, validator, service, scanner, provider adapter, storage behavior, dependency, route, or M1-05 integration.

#### Decision Review Contract

**Decision owner and authority.** The project owner supplied the exact presentation baseline and explicitly requested its linkage and implementation. OD-027 records that accepted direction. The primary remains sole writer for requirements, decisions, roadmap, specification, plan and status authorities.

**Decision.** Use the owner-selected evidence-first contract rather than either the previous metadata-heavy interface or its technical-disclosure revision. Keep every canonical field and complete collection inside the validated `PageAnalysisRun`; omit run/scan/provider/native plumbing from provider-independent primary Results; derive one truthful overview; map retained evidence into human-readable Finding and manual-review presentations; preserve valid-zero/failure separation, inert text, async ownership and later provider-specific disclosure.

**Options reviewed.** A retained metadata-first view was rejected as too dense. A progressive-disclosure view was rejected because it still made technical mechanics a primary interaction. The selected presentation removes those mechanics from the primary UI while preserving them internally and makes deterministic evidence the selected-detail content. A dashboard, score, chart, recent-run browser, settings page, model manager, preview, or generalized diagnostic UI is outside scope.

**Binding review questions.** A read-only `decision_analyst` must verify that: (1) omitted fields remain canonical rather than deleted; (2) every Finding and ScannerReviewObservation remains represented exactly once; (3) `Groq API URL` means only already-known fixed-adapter configuration and does not revive `REQ-LLM-017`; (4) selection and Analyze perform no provider probe and missing configuration does not block the scan; (5) trusted-input enforcement remains owned by admission/security documentation although its notice is not visible; (6) later provider-relevant disclosure, invocation provenance and abstention no-call meaning remain intact; (7) result content and the shared live region communicate lifecycle without a duplicate visible status field; and (8) the exact presentation document, requirements, roadmap, SPEC-001, ADR clarifications and this plan are mutually consistent. `PASS` or `PASS WITH FOLLOW-UPS` with every material item resolved permits TDD preflight. `REVISE`, `BLOCKED`, or a changed product boundary stops implementation.

**OD-027 cumulative invariant packet.** This packet is complete for the presentation replacement and does not silently reuse the older `I4.1`–`I4.9` literals. Re-run every row after a material authority correction, after Green, and at S3 review. A material failure blocks the next dependent stage.

| ID | Trigger / fixture | Required result | Evidence / responsible reviewer |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| P8.1 Canonical retention | Completed and failed `PageAnalysisRun` records containing run, scan, provider, coverage and native evidence fields omitted from primary UI | Validators and records remain unchanged; omission is presentation only and downstream provider/provenance data remains available to later owning tasks. | Authority diff plus unchanged domain/validator hashes; decision analyst, R3 checkpoint and implementation critical reviewer. |
| P8.2 Complete projection | Rich completion with every Finding and ScannerReviewObservation, including repeated same-rule items | Each Finding appears exactly once in one human-readable check group and each observation exactly once under **Needs manual review**; no sampling, truncation, deduplication or status aliasing. | Focused browser assertions and collection counts; test worker, frontend worker and implementation critical reviewer. |
| P8.3 Fixed configuration | Local selected with known missing model; Groq selected with known missing fixed-adapter API URL | Show only the exact selected-mode configuration message; no user-entered endpoint, provider probe, synthetic request, scan block, ProviderInvocation or fallback. `REQ-LLM-017` remains Deferred. | Controlled collaborator-call counts and network isolation; test worker and implementation critical reviewer. |
| P8.4 Trusted-input boundary | Ready form, malformed/credential/scheme input, valid trusted public HTTPS input and completed Results | No supported-use notice in ready form or Results; admission still rejects invalid inputs before a run/navigation; documentation retains authorization/trust/unsupported-target limitations and public claims remain bounded. | `HS-001`, admission regressions, visible-copy exclusions and authority review; test worker, R3 checkpoint and implementation critical reviewer. |
| P8.5 Provider semantics | Provider-independent completion, later eligible selected-Finding generation, abstention, attempted failure and provenance states | Scan Results omit mode/provider/model/no-call text. Later owning workflow shows provider/model/egress context before explicit Generate; abstention records no invocation; attempts retain bounded provenance; no implicit call or fallback. | Authority trace now; later M3 tests for provider states; R3 checkpoint and later provider-workflow reviewer. |
| P8.6 Truthful lifecycle | Start, rich completion, valid zero, failed outcome, replacement attempt and stale settlement | One shared live region announces start and truthful terminal outcome without a visible status field; failure never becomes zero/partial success or replace prior accepted evidence; focus remains predictable. | Focused async/announcement/focus tests and Narrator smoke; test worker, frontend worker and implementation critical reviewer. |
| P8.7 Evidence-first presentation | Image-alt, label and color-contrast Findings with unavailable facts plus native incomplete observations | One overview precedes complete groups; selected detail shows only direct rule-specific human-readable evidence; internal IDs, raw rule/check/message keys and technical disclosures stay absent; unavailable facts remain honest. | Focused DOM/text assertions, inert-string cases, wide/narrow/200% visual evidence; frontend worker and implementation critical reviewer. |
| P8.8 Accessibility and reflow | Keyboard traversal/selection/return, 320 × 800, 1280 × 720, browser-menu 200%, long URLs/locators/colors/ratios | Semantic headings/names/relationships, visible focus, non-color distinctions, logical narrow order, wrapping without horizontal page scroll, and no keyboard trap. | Axe plus keyboard/reflow automation, retained screenshots and heard Narrator evidence; implementation critical reviewer and different integrated reviewer. |
| P8.9 Authority and scope | Complete current diff, linked presentation contract, roadmap/status/spec/ADR set and generated-boundary inventory | Current authorities agree; historical clauses are explicitly superseded; M1-05/service/scanner/storage/provider adapters/dependencies remain unchanged; actual links resolve; no Git commit/push. | `git diff --check`, local link and status checks, path/lease receipts; R3 checkpoint, primary and integrated reviewer. |

**Research Assignment Capsule v1 — `M104-OD027-DECISION-AUDIT-001`.** Tier `R3`; trigger: the owner-selected presentation amends accepted provider-disclosure, trusted-input-copy, canonical-state, evidence-completeness and screen-reader contracts across multiple requirement modules. Budget: one read-only `decision_analyst` audit and one bounded follow-up using the same instance; no discovery pass, researcher, drafter or parallel report; one later fresh `critical_research_reviewer` pre-implementation checkpoint only if the analyst finds the primary-authored authority artifact materially incomplete. Exact question and target answer are the eight binding review questions above and `P8.1`–`P8.9`, applied to the complete current authority diff and returning `DRAFT READY`, `RETURN FOR RESEARCH`, or `OWNER DIRECTION` with severity-ranked traceable findings. Shared frozen evidence IDs are the clean HEAD `7726864`, the pre-OD-027 source/test hashes recorded in `M104-RESULTS-TRUSTED-INPUT-S3-CORRECTION-07-FULL-001`, and current documentation diff identity to be recomputed by the analyst; implementation evidence is explicitly stale for the new visible contract. Hard gates and forbidden scope are the eight questions, `P8.1`–`P8.9`, plus no canonical-data deletion, endpoint/profile capability, provider probe, scan blocking, M1-05 behavior, new dependency, or authority write by the analyst. #### Milestone Assignment Packet v2

**Objective and acceptance contract.** Replace the current rendered Analyze and Results interface with every observable behavior in [Analyze and results presentation](../ui/ANALYZE_AND_RESULTS_PRESENTATION.md). Before analysis, show only the compact form task, no default mode and no normal disclosure. After an invalid submission, associate exact URL/mode messages with their controls. When supplied known configuration flags say the Local model or fixed Groq-adapter API URL is absent, show only the exact nonblocking selected-mode message and make no probe. Announce `Analysis started.`, `Analysis completed: N findings and M items need manual review.`, or a safe failure without moving focus. Show Results only after a terminal outcome. For completion, show analyzed page, exact limitation, one complete overview, all three user-facing groups, every human-labeled Finding, direct rule-specific evidence, focus return, and every manual-review observation. For valid zero and failure, render only the accepted state-specific contract. Omit every field listed by the presentation contract from the primary interface while retaining validated input unchanged.

**Canonical states.** Parent runs remain exactly `running`, `completed`, and `failed`; Findings remain canonical `unprocessed`; native `incomplete` remains `ScannerReviewObservation`. The UI derives words such as **needs review** and **manual review** for presentation only and creates no second durable state. A completed zero is possible only after complete exact-three-rule coverage; malformed coverage remains rejected by existing admission. A failed attempt never replaces previously completed evidence.

**Responsibility and dependency placement.** `App.tsx` retains async reservation, collaborator settlement, immutable admitted run ownership, result replacement, selection and shared announcement state and passes only validated records and explicit known-configuration input downward. `RunControls.tsx` owns form fields, validation, exact missing-configuration feedback and normalized intent construction. `RunResults.tsx` owns completed/failed result composition, complete collection projection, selection wiring and user-facing group order. A bounded local extraction under `src/client/` is permitted only for the present responsibilities of results overview and rule-specific evidence when that materially improves cohesion; it must depend only on React plus domain types and remain private to Results. `styles.css` owns the compact responsive Evidence Light presentation. Test/harness code may expose explicit deterministic configuration flags only; it must not simulate a provider probe.

**Reuse dispositions.** `App` — `EXTEND`; `TargetAnalysisForm` — `EXTEND`; existing admission and immutable detach logic — `REUSE_AS_IS`; `RunResults` — `REFACTORED` because its current technical-run, raw-coverage and nested-technical-evidence responsibilities are intentionally removed from visible composition and replaced by present overview/list/evidence responsibilities; `styles.css` — `EXTEND`; existing fixture builders and canonical record types — `REUSE_AS_IS`. A local overview or evidence component is `EXTRACT_LOCAL` only if the implementation worker records the exact cohesive responsibility and re-runs focused checks after the refactor. No shared component library, state hook, schema, adapter, configuration service or generic evidence engine may be created.

**Test ownership and accepted boundary.** One fresh `test_worker` owns only `tests/target-results-ui.test.ts` and `tests/helpers/m104-ui-harness.ts`. It must preserve the existing admission, detachment, stale-result, overlapping-operation, inert-text, complete-collection, valid-zero/failure, focus, axe and 320-pixel behavioral boundaries while replacing assertions that freeze OD-027-superseded copy or technical disclosures. It must add direct checks for initial no-selection/no-helper state; exact associated mode validation; both known missing-configuration messages with successful provider-independent Analyze; hidden ready/result provider metadata; exact overview/group labels and counts; human-readable labels; rule-specific direct evidence; manual-review tasks; omitted internal/native fields; failure-only content; completion and selection announcements; and no item truncation. The test count may change only when required to express one coherent new contract; no assertion may be skipped or conditional on absent production behavior. The helper may accept deterministic configuration flags and must preserve owned-origin isolation and teardown.

**Frontend ownership.** After accepted Red, one separate `frontend_code_worker` owns only `src/client/App.tsx`, `src/client/RunControls.tsx`, `src/client/RunResults.tsx`, `src/client/styles.css`, and at most two newly extracted private `src/client/` presentation components explicitly named in its handoff. The accepted test/helper identities are forbidden during Green. The worker reaches minimum Green, then records `RETAINED`, `REFACTORED`, or `RECONCILE` against the actual changed surface. It may perform only a behavior-preserving local extraction allowed above.

**Frontend visual capsule.** Wide sample: 1280 × 720. Narrow sample: 320 × 800. Human sample: controlled Chromium at browser-menu 200% zoom. The overview stays above a bounded Findings list and flexible evidence detail; narrow order is overview, Findings, selected evidence, manual review. URLs, locators, colors and ratios wrap without horizontal page scrolling. Signal Blue is limited to primary action, links, focus and selection. Caution styling accompanies exact manual-review text. No nested technical cards, oversized hero, score, chart, badge system, hover-only content, truncation, or animation. Real-browser evidence must cover ready form, validation, rich completion, selected color evidence, manual review, valid zero, failed analysis, keyboard return, 320-pixel reflow, actual 200% reflow and one heard Narrator smoke path.

**Commands and generated boundaries.** Use the existing pinned Node/npm and task-owned Chromium runtime. Focused UI command: `npm.cmd --cache temp/m104-setup/npm-cache --no-audit --no-fund --ignore-scripts run test:focused -- --test-timeout=120000 tests/target-results-ui.test.ts`. Independent checks: `npm.cmd --cache temp/m104-setup/npm-cache --no-audit --no-fund --ignore-scripts run typecheck` and `npm.cmd --cache temp/m104-setup/npm-cache --no-audit --no-fund --ignore-scripts run build -- --configLoader native`. The complete sequential barrier remains the five existing backend/scanner suites (290 tests), then the accepted UI suite, strict typecheck and native-loader build. Browser operations own only `m104-browser-runtime/browsers`, `temp/m104-setup`, `temp/m104-ui`, `temp/m103-scan`, `node_modules/.vite-temp`, `dist/client`, and explicitly named screenshots. All must be reviewed and then removed at closure without touching shared caches or unrelated files.

**Budgets and stops.** One test preflight, one coherent Red, one separate frontend Green/Refactor, at most one same-contract correction per role, one S3 correction loop, and one no-diff stop per write role. Stop on changed canonical data, missing or duplicated collection items, a provider probe, blocking scan on provider configuration, user-entered endpoint behavior, dropped trusted-input admission, executable external content, changed M1-05/service/storage/scanner scope, invalid Red, wrong file, lease violation, repeated decisive failure, two no-diff handoffs, or exhausted correction budget. No commit or push is authorized.

**Review and closure.** After the complete barrier, a brand-new `critical_reviewer` inspects the full changed surface, contract mapping, security/provider boundaries, accessibility, responsive evidence and cohesion. After accepted actual browser/Narrator evidence, a different fresh `independent_reviewer` performs integrated closure review. Primary then runs link/authority/status/diff checks, removes only exact generated roots, independently verifies absence, updates all current-status authorities, and may mark M1-04 Complete and archive this plan only when every gate passes.
